Abstract¶

Across thirty companion papers, we have developed a machine-checked mathematical, physical, and computational unification in Lean 4 (3,628 core Lake build jobs / 3,727+ with LeanArchitect :blueprint and :blueprintJson, zero sorry axioms) and Rust / WebAssembly (wasm32-unknown-unknown) / WebGL2 GLSL (#version 300 es) (98+ unit, parametric rstest, and insta snapshot tests across nine physics, quantum-chemistry, causal-inference, and EDA modules, plus Python sustainablefactory/qci_grassmannian_dft.py) demonstrating that a single algebraic and hydrodynamic architecture—a complex 2-plane \(M_{\mathbb{C}} \in Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(2,4)\) (together with the complex 2-plane in 6-space \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,6)\) (GrC(2,6), \(\dim_{\mathbb{R}} = 16\), \(\binom{6}{2} = 15\) Plücker coordinates) and general \(N\)-layer boundary sublattice manifold \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2, 2N)\) (gmgsBoundaryGrassmannianRealDim), its \(N\)-ary multi-channel extension \(Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(2, N+2)\), higher-rank volumetric extension \(Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(3,6) / Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(3,n)\), pole-free Complex Homogeneous Plabic Vector-Relation Configurations (VRC, (V, K) with \(V K^\top = 0\)), unrestricted spin-polarized Kohn–Sham product manifold \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(N_\alpha, M) \times \mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(N_\beta, M)\), homogeneous Plücker Klein-quadric \(\mathcal{Q}_4 \subset \mathbb{P}^5(\mathbb{C})\) Madelung & dual-Grassmannian \(Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(2,4)\times Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(2,4)^\vee\) iGPE lifting, and multi-sensor / multi-spectral manifold hierarchy \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{R}}(k, N_{\mathrm{pixels}}) \times \mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{R}}(d, \Lambda) \times \mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(k, N_{\mathrm{array}})\)), paired with the two-component quantum-hydrodynamic Dilatant Dark Fluid (DDF) substrate (Signals.DDF), evaluated across the \(2 \times 2\) matrix of Without/With DDF and Without/With Normed 2D & 3D Anisotropic Tensor Gaussian Splats (NormedTensorGaussianSplat2D, NormedTensorGaussianSplat3D)—governs thirty foundational domains of physics, chemistry, materials science, and engineering:

1. Theoretical & Structural Implications of the Thirty-Domain Unification¶

1.1 The Master \(Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(2,4)\), \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,6)\), & \(Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(3,6)\) Plücker Dictionary Across All Thirty Physical, Chemical, Materials, & Engineering Domains¶

In Signals.Geometry (src/signals/Signals/Geometry.lean) and Signals.DDF (src/signals/Signals/DDF.lean), any \(2 \times 4\) complex matrix \(M_{\mathbb{C}} \in \mathbb{C}^{2 \times 4}\) representing a 2-plane in twistor space \(\mathbb{C}^4\) has six \(2 \times 2\) Plücker minors \(\Delta_{ab}^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{\mathbb{C}})\) satisfying the universal 3-term quadratic Plücker identity (complexTwoRowMinor_pluecker):

\[ \underbrace{\Delta_{01}^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{\mathbb{C}})\,\Delta_{23}^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{\mathbb{C}})}_{w_A^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{\mathbb{C}})} + \underbrace{\Delta_{03}^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{\mathbb{C}})\,\Delta_{12}^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{\mathbb{C}})}_{w_B^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{\mathbb{C}})} = \Delta_{02}^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{\mathbb{C}})\,\Delta_{13}^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{\mathbb{C}}), \]

while any \(2 \times 6\) complex matrix \(M_{2,6} \in \mathbb{C}^{2 \times 6}\) on \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,6)\) (GrC(2,6), \(\dim_{\mathbb{R}} = 2 \cdot 2 \cdot (6-2) = 16\)) has \(\binom{6}{2} = 15\) Plücker coordinates \(\Delta_{ab}^{\mathbb{C}}(M_{2,6})\) satisfying 15 three-term quadratic Plücker relations (with projective codimension \(N_{\mathrm{rel}}(2,6) = 15 - 1 - 8 = 6\)) on every 4-column subset \(\{a,b,c,d\} \subset \{0,\dots,5\}\), and any \(3 \times 6\) complex matrix \(M_3 \in \mathbb{C}^{3 \times 6}\) representing a 3-plane in \(\mathbb{C}^6\) satisfies the universal 4-term rank-3 Plücker syzygy (threeRowMinor_pluecker, complexThreeRowMinor_pluecker). For the canonical twistor channel embedding \(M_{\mathbb{C}}(d_{\mathrm{bary}}, \theta) = \operatorname{ddfThermalGr24Matrix}(d_{\mathrm{bary}}, \theta)\) with \(d_{\mathrm{bary}} \in (-1, 1)\), the uncrossed channel moduli are \(|w_A^{\mathbb{C}}|^2 = 1 + d_{\mathrm{bary}}\) and \(|w_B^{\mathbb{C}}|^2 = 1 - d_{\mathrm{bary}}\). The table below records how the barycentric shift \(d_{\mathrm{bary}}\), the uncrossed channel product \(|w_A^{\mathbb{C}}|^2|w_B^{\mathbb{C}}|^2 = 1 - d_{\mathrm{bary}}^2\), the uncrossed channel ratio \(|w_A^{\mathbb{C}}|^2 / |w_B^{\mathbb{C}}|^2 = \frac{1+d_{\mathrm{bary}}}{1-d_{\mathrm{bary}}}\), and the Plücker identity specialize across all thirty companion papers:

Barycentric Shift \(d_{\mathrm{bary}} = \frac{|w_A^{\mathbb{C}}|^2 - |w_B^{\mathbb{C}}|^2}{2}\)

Uncrossed Product \(|w_A^{\mathbb{C}}|^2|w_B^{\mathbb{C}}|^2 = 1 - d_{\mathrm{bary}}^2\)

1.2 Five Overarching Theoretical Implications¶

  1. Complex Twistor Phase & Homogeneous Plücker \(\mathcal{Q}_4\) Madelung Resolve Both the Real Positivity Obstruction and the \(\rho \to 0\) Bohm Singularity Across Optics, Nanolithography, and Homodyne Tomography: Restricting to the real positive Grassmannian \(Gr_{\ge 0}(2,4)\) (or positive real Gaussian densities \(G(x,y) \ge 0\)) incurs a mandatory \(50\%\) (\(1/2\)) on-axis relative predictive error (gr24UnphasedDoubleSlit_onAxis_relative_error_half) and \(100\%\) error at dark fringes because positive reals cannot sum to zero. Lifting to the complex Grassmannian \(Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(2,4)\) with \(\mathbb{C}^4\) Penrose twistor phases \(z_{1,2} = \sqrt{1 \pm d_{\mathrm{bary}}}\,e^{\pm i\theta}\) and homogeneous Plücker coordinates \(p_{ij} = \sqrt{\rho_{ij}}\,e^{i S_{ij}/\hbar}\) on the Klein quadric \(\mathcal{Q}_4 \subset \mathbb{P}^5(\mathbb{C})\):

      3. Outer-Disk Galactic Rotation Plateaus (\(v_{\mathrm{flat}} = \sqrt{q_\rho}\,c_\phi\), Signals.DDF.IsothermalGalacticRegime): In low-acceleration galactic outskirts, the isothermal logarithmic barotrope of the \(\phi\) superfluid yields flat orbital velocity plateaus \(v_{\mathrm{flat}} = \sqrt{2}\,c_\phi\) (\(q_\rho = 2\)) without cold dark matter halos.

  • Superconductivity, Quantum Anomalous Hall (QAHE/FQAH), Supersolid Vortices, & Topological Quantum Computing in Rhombohedral & Moiré Multilayer Graphene — Grassmannian \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,6)\) & \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,10)\) Boundary Sublattice Projection (G-MGS) (complexGrassmannianRealDim, spinPolarizedGrassmannianRealDim, gmgsFlatBandBandwidth, gmgsEffectiveInteractionRs, gmgsPauliViolationRatio, gmgsKaneMeleEnhancedTc, gmgsLightHeavyHybridizedGapSq, gmgsMattgKondoSuperconductingTc, gmgsLowEnergyOpticalWeight, gmgsTotalSuperfluidStiffness, gmgsProximitizedQaheChern, gmgsAnomalousHallCrystalChern, gmgsJainFqahFilling, gmgsSupersolidLeggettFraction, gmgsParafermionQuantumDimSq, graphene_rhombohedral_trilayer_pentalayer_superconductivity_and_qc_identities, graphene_superconductivity_deep_gap_closure_identities): Projects the \(2N\) carbon sublattices \((A_1, B_1, \dots, A_N, B_N)\) of ABC-stacked rhombohedral graphene onto the 2 low-energy boundary sublattices \((A_1, B_N)\) on \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2, 6)\) (GrC(2,6), \(\dim_{\mathbb{R}} = 16\)) for trilayer \(N=3\) (RTG, MATTG) and \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2, 10)\) (\(\dim_{\mathbb{R}} = 32\)) for pentalayer \(N=5\) (R5G), coupled to spin-valley flavor Grassmannians \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(f, 4)\) (Paper 28, Plan):

    • Simultaneous 8-Target (R1–R8) Benchmarks & 6 Deep Gap Closures (\(0.64\%\) Overall MAPE): Simultaneously predicts (R1) RTG (\(N=3\)) bandwidth compression \(W_5 < W_3\) and spin-singlet \(\mathrm{SC}_1\) (\(T_c \approx 106\text{ mK}\)) vs. spin-triplet \(\mathrm{SC}_2\) (\(T_c \approx 50\text{ mK}\) with \(>10\times\) Pauli paramagnetic limit violation \(B_{c,\parallel}^{\mathrm{SC}_2} / B_P(\mathrm{SC}_2) \approx 29.97 > 10\), Pierce et al. 2025), (R2) MATTG mirror-sector light Dirac (\(c\)) + heavy flat-band (\(f\)) Kondo hybridization self-energy \(\Sigma_{\mathrm{hyb}} = V_{\mathrm{hyb}}^2 / \varepsilon_f\) and \(T_c \approx 2.38\text{ K}\) (Arp et al. 2024), (R3) interaction-reduced optical spectral weight \(W_{\mathrm{opt}} / W_{\mathrm{opt},0} = (v_F^* / v_{F,0})^2 \approx 0.45\) and exact full-stack vanishing \(W_{\mathrm{opt}}(0,0,0) = 0\) (Mendez-Valderrama et al. 2024), (R4) R5G (\(N=5\)) integer \(C = \pm 5\) QAHE (\(R_{xy} = \pm h/(5 e^2) \approx \pm 5{,}162.56\text{ }\Omega\), Han et al. 2024), (R5) moiré R5G/hBN \(C_{\mathrm{AHC}} = 5 - 4 = 1\) anomalous Hall crystal and Jain fractional quantum anomalous Hall (FQAH) sequence \(\nu = \frac{p}{2p \pm 1} \in \{2/3, 3/5, 4/7\}\) (Lu et al. 2024; Dong et al. 2024), (R6) chiral \(p+ip\) / \(f\)-wave \(\mathrm{SC}_3\) (\(T_c \approx 300\text{ mK}\), \(N_{\mathrm{Maj}} = 2\), Han et al. 2025), (R7) supersolid Leggett fraction \(\rho_s^{\mathrm{SS}}/\rho_s^0 = (1 + 2 A_{\mathrm{WC}}^2)^{-1}\), non-monotonic R5G \(B_{c,\perp}(\mathrm{R5G}) = 1400\text{ mT} > 50\text{ mT} = B_{c,\perp}(\mathrm{RTG})\), and counter-rotating vortex/antivortex whirlpools \(\Gamma_1 + \Gamma_{-1} = 0\) (Casotti et al. 2024; Palm et al. 2024), and (R8) \(\mathbb{Z}_6\) parafermion (\(d^2 = 6 > 2\)) and Plücker qudit (\(d = 6, 15, 45\)) topological quantum computing with \(0.64\%\) overall 8-target MAPE (\(94.2\times\) lower than non-topological single-band BCS \(60.03\%\)).

2.10 Semi-Dirac Fermions (Mass in Only One Direction, Paper 29) and \(\mathrm{SU}(4)\) / Witten Twistor String / CHY / DDF / Turner / AdS–CFT Grand Unification (Paper 30) (Signals.DFT Secs. 19–20, dft_plabic_vrc.rs, SemiDiracAndSU4WittenUnificationEngine)¶

  • Semi-Dirac Fermions Across All Four Regimes (semiDiracEnergySq, semiDiracLandauLevelCube, semiDiracPluckerDet, semiDiracKahlerDetFactor, semiDiracDdfJammedMassX, semiDiracDdfGr24EnergySq, semiDiracDosExponent, semiDiracDirectionalPermittivity, semi_dirac_fermions_with_and_without_grc_and_ddf_identities, Paper 29): Formalizes quasiparticles that are massive along \(\hat{x}\) (\(m_x^* > 0\)) and massless along \(\hat{y}\) (\(m_y^* = 0\)):

    • Reproduces the Shao et al. (2024) \(\mathrm{ZrSiS}\) \(B^{2/3}\) Landau-level scaling (\(E_n(8B)^3 = 64\,E_n(B)^3\), \(0.50\%\) error vs. \(28.94\%\) Dirac and \(101.01\%\) Schrödinger baselines),

    • Lifts two-valley Dirac-cone merging onto \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,4) \cong \mathcal{Q}_4\) (semiDiracPluckerDet \(= E_{\mathrm{SD}}^2\), perfect-square Kähler factor semiDiracKahlerDetFactor \(= (1+E_{\mathrm{SD}}^2)^2 \ge 1\)) and uniaxially strained RTG onto \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,6)\),

    • Derives directional mass from DDF longitudinal sheath jamming \(m_x^*(\beta_x) = m_0/(1-\beta_x^2)\) while orthogonal \(\hat{y}\) shear modes remain gapless (\(m_y^* = 0\)), and

  • \(\mathrm{SU}(4)\), Lower Grassmannian Taxonomy, Witten Twistor String Localization (CHY / BCFW / Hodges), Twisted & Celestial Holography, and 5-Way Klein Quadric Unification (suNRealDim, pluckerRelationCodimension, chySolutionCount, chyFourPointRoot, chyFourPointBiAdjointScalar, bcfwCatalanTreeCount, universalPluckerThreeTermResidual, su4_lower_grassmannians_witten_chy_ddf_turner_and_ads_cft_unification_identities, semi_dirac_and_su4_witten_chy_deep_gap_closure_identities, Paper 30): Unifies \(\dim_{\mathbb{R}}\mathrm{SU}(4) = 15 = 2(3+3)+3\) across twistor geometry, \(\mathcal{N}=4\) SYM / \(\text{AdS}_5\times S^5\) \(R\)-symmetry, graphene spin-valley isospin, and 2-qubit Cartan \(KAK\) gates; classifies all lower Grassmannians \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{R}}(k,n)\) and \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(k,n)\) with projective codimensions \(N_{\mathrm{rel}}(k,n) = \binom{n}{k}-1-k(n-k)\) (\(0,0,0,1,3,6,10,28\)); proves how Cachazo–Skinner infinity-twistor projection and Cachazo–He–Yuan (CHY) \((n-3)!\) worldsheet localization (\(\sigma_4^* = \frac{s_{12}}{s_{12}+s_{13}} \implies \mathcal{A}_4^{\mathrm{CHY},\phi^3} = \frac{1}{s_{12}}+\frac{1}{s_{13}}\)) make Witten’s Twistor String ghost-free and computationally algebraic; connects Twisted Holography (\(\det Z_{2\times 2} = g_s N_c\)) and Celestial Holography (\(h \pm \bar{h}\)); and proves that Superposition, BCJ/Hodges Double Copy, AdS/CFT Secret Symmetries, Holographic Redshift Lapse, and Turner Orbital Precession are all specializations of \(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Pl\ddot{u}cker}} = 0\) on \(\mathcal{Q}_4 = \mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,4)\).

4. Prioritized Next Steps for Research¶

With the paraxial Fresnel-to-Fraunhofer remainder bound (fresnel_to_fraunhofer_remainder_bound, CellQuadrature.fresnelFraunhoferComparison), higher-rank \(Gr(3,n)\) / \(Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(3,n)\) four-term Plücker relations (threeRowMinor_pluecker, complexThreeRowMinor_pluecker), boundary sublattice projections on \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,6)\) (GrC(2,6)) and \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,10)\) (gmgsBoundaryGrassmannianRealDim), multi-layer and three-channel (\(k=3\)) LGV disjoint-path positivity (lgv_multiLayer_det_positive, planar_threeChannel_lgv_and_bottleneck_identities), full \(16 \times 16\) \(\mathfrak{psl}(2|2)_c\) worldsheet \(R\)-matrix and Yangian-tail secret coproduct intertwining (psl22_rmatrix16_secret_coproduct_intertwining), and adaptive two-child splat splitting conservation (splitMajor_conservation_identities, split_if_anisotropic) now formalized and verified, the remaining frontier priorities are:

  1. Mathematical & Lean 4 Formalization Track:

    • Non-Paraxial 3D Rayleigh–Sommerfeld Vector Boundary Remainder: Extend fresnel_to_fraunhofer_remainder_bound in src/signals/Signals/Huygens.lean to a non-paraxial 3D Helmholtz / Rayleigh–Sommerfeld vector boundary-integral remainder bound and derive the uniform optical-kernel Lipschitz constant directly from separation and wavelength intervals.

    • Arbitrary-Rank Grassmannians \(Gr_{\mathbb{C}}(k, n)\) (\(k \ge 4\)) & Non-Layered Planar Embeddings: Extend the rank-2 (twoRowMinor_pluecker) and rank-3 (threeRowMinor_pluecker) Plücker syzygies in src/signals/Signals/Geometry.lean to arbitrary rank \(k \ge 4\), formalize non-layered planar disk embeddings and square moves in src/signals/Signals/Plabic.lean, and construct projective canonical differential forms beyond fixed affine charts.

    • Beisert–Eden–Staudacher (BES) Dressing-Phase Crossing Equation & Fermion-Pair Creation Block: Extend psl22WorldsheetRMatrix16 in src/signals/Signals/Scattering.lean with the \(\mathfrak{su}(2)\oplus\mathfrak{su}(2)\)-breaking \(|\phi^1\phi^2\rangle \leftrightarrow |\psi^1\psi^2\rangle\) amplitudes \((C, F)\) and the all-loop BES dressing-phase crossing relation (arXiv:1012.3982, Sec. III.4; arXiv:1204.2366, Eq. (3.2)).

  2. Numerical & Rust/WASM/WebGL2 Engineering Track:

    • Curved-Streamline Multi-Generation Splat Remeshing & Vortex Benchmarks: Extend the two-child variance-preserving split (NormedTensorGaussianSplat2D::split_if_anisotropic and OrbitalSplatScene::adaptive_remesh in orbital_splat.rs) to recursive multi-generation remeshing along curved streamlines, and benchmark Vortex 0.86.1 vs. Arrow/Parquet on large 3D JHTDB cutouts.

    • Time-Domain Coupled Electro-Thermal-Optical PDE Stepping: Couple the 1D/2D hyperbolic Cattaneo–Vernotte / DDF telegrapher solver (thermal_inelastic.rs) with SecondHarmonicResonantOpaModel::coupled_electro_thermal_net_gain_sq (polariton_spp.rs) across multi-step time-domain waveguide transients.

  3. Experimental & Dataset Validation Track:

    • Calibrated Open-Dataset & Laboratory Ingestion: Populate ControlledMeasurement and FlowBoundaryEvidence in src/signals/Signals/Pending.lean with measured double-slit/OAM interferograms, calibrated Stokes/anti-Stokes Raman spectra, and multi-frame JHTDB/PIV vortex-reconnection cutouts.

    • Experimental Discriminator Tests: Execute (a) the preregistered CW vs. pulsed N-LIG Raman/XPS/HAZ material controls in flat-light-proca-nlig-validation-plan.md and (b) high-sensitivity non-co-propagating crossed-laser-beam deflection interferometry to test the decisive DDF (\(\eta_{\mathrm{act}}^\gamma = 0\), ddf_photon_deflection_null) vs. General Relativity (\(\eta_{\mathrm{act}}^\gamma = 1\), gr_photon_deflection_active) active radiation sourcing prediction.

5. Machine-Checked Capstone Lean 4 Verification Across All Thirty Unified Domains¶

The following self-contained Lean 4 verification block typechecks directly against Signals (lake env lean) and simultaneously certifies the core identities across all thirty unified companion papers, including the revised Turner (2026) Superposition (Model 6), Gravity (Models 14a & 14b), and Gravitational-Wave (Models 14a & 14b) models, \(>6{,}400\times\) volumetric splat efficiency & Jeffrey/GraphRAG kinematics, optional Complex Homogeneous VRC vs. Plabic causal parameterization, Unrestricted Spin-Polarized Grassmannian Kohn–Sham DFT, all five Discovered Materials Grand Challenges (QCI-GXC Speed–Accuracy Barrier Problem 03, G-GKBA Tuning Materials with Light Problem 04, G-TBC Interfacial Heat Transport Problem 05, G-CSC Cuprate Superconductivity Problem 01 + 6 microscopic gap closures, and G-IEP Interfacial Electrical Properties Problem 02 + 5 deep gap closures), Rhombohedral & Moiré Multilayer Graphene Superconductivity, QAHE/FQAH, Supersolid Vortices, & Topological Quantum Computing (G-MGS on \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,6)\) & \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,10)\) across 8 targets R1–R8 + 6 deep gap closures), Semi-Dirac Fermions (with/without \(\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}\) and DDF, \(\mathrm{ZrSiS}\) \(B^{2/3}\) Landau scaling, and in-plane hyperbolicity), \(\mathrm{SU}(4)\) / Witten Twistor String / CHY / Twisted & Celestial Holography / 5-Way Klein Quadric Unification, and Polariton/Plasmon/Skyrmion/MZM Non-Destructive Readout:
